Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The linear variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 911 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 683 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to linear theory, we decompose the problem into 911 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 683. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
